# 生成、共同实现与伦理：完整理论链对照表 / Generativity, co-realization and ethics: the connected theory

内容更新 / Content updated: **2026-10-08** · Asia/Bangkok · **94 条导航项 / 94 indexed entries**

[总成果目录 / Results catalogue](README.md) · [全部章节 / All chapters](ARGUMENTS-A-L.md) · [后半章节 / More chapters](ARGUMENTS-M-Z.md) · [Lean 声明 / Lean declarations](LEAN-DECLARATIONS.md)

本表覆盖你指定的完整理论链：生成根基、共同实现、生生伦理、情境判断、学习、良知与智慧，以及相关证明、评审和来源。它按论证关系整理，完整列出这条链的主要命题与支撑入口；不把整个仓库的其他理论分支或每个辅助引理逐行混入。精细章节与声明可由总成果目录继续查阅。

This table covers the requested connected chain: generative grounding, joint realization, Shengsheng ethics, situated judgment, learning, Liangzhi and wisdom, together with proofs, reviews and sources. It organizes the chain’s main propositions and supporting entries by their argumentative roles; it does not mix in every other repository branch or every auxiliary lemma. The wider catalogue provides the finer chapter and declaration inventory.

## 核心论断 / The central claim

> 新形成的关系使原来不能共同发生的生活与进一步形成条件能够共同实现时，整体获得了新的实现方式；其正面意义可以就在这种共同形成中，而不必先变成发起者的收益。

> When newly formed relations enable forms of living and conditions for further formation that could not previously occur together to be jointly realized, the whole acquires a new mode of realization; its favorable significance may lie in this co-formation itself, without first becoming a return to its initiator.

这段话是 AI 撰写的候选论证及其英文对译；共同形成的某些机制与表示已得到检查，其普遍伦理识别仍在论证。 / This is an AI-authored candidate argument and its English translation. Some mechanisms and representations of joint formation are checked; its universal ethical identification remains under investigation.

[所引论证与完整范围 / Quoted argument and full scope](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-INTEGRATION.md#4-正面伦理推论得到更准确的对象但仍需要根据)

## 主链与读法 / Chain and reading guide

同一生成展开 → 组织及后续条件 → 有支持的共同实现 → 整体质量与真实得失 → 情境理由 → 学习、关怀、良知与智慧 → 实践及校准。这是阅读主线，不是已完成的单向推导；组织—调节—学习—关切的形成路径与规范研究相互回接。各连接的根据和完成程度分列于表中。

One unfolding → organization and further conditions → supported co-realization → whole quality and real gains/losses → situated reasons → learning, concern, Liangzhi and wisdom → practice and calibration. This is a reading guide, not a completed one-way derivation; organization–regulation–learning–concern and normative inquiry reconnect with one another. The tables distinguish each connection’s warrant and completion scope.

表中的理论、方法、机制、命题与检查包是不同维护单元，并非同等地位的独立理论。‘已检查’仅及于所列定义、前提、对应与历史版本；‘候选’和‘未完成’保留其真实研究状态。

Theories, methods, mechanisms, propositions and verification packages are different maintenance units, not equally established independent theories. Checked results retain their definitions, hypotheses, correspondence and source versions; candidates and open questions remain identified.

## 一、共同根基与正面论断 / Shared grounding and the positive claim

| C01 | 同一生成展开与关系分化<br>One generative unfolding and relational differentiation | 活动形成并转化后续活动的条件；组织、认识、行动与评价在同一展开中分化，并通过实际相连的机制及其对应得到说明。 系统通过实际关系辨认，边界须保留相关依存；预测充分性与构成参与分别说明。 | Activity forms and transforms conditions of further activity; organization, knowing, action and evaluation differentiate within one unfolding and are explained through connected mechanisms and their correspondence. | C03 | 生生不息的正面方向<br>Shengsheng as a positive direction | 整体创造更多生、更丰富的可能与多样性，并使复杂组织继续发展的能力，是正在论证的善的方向。 | The whole’s formation of more life, richer possibilities and diversity, and capacities for further organizational development is the proposed favorable direction. | Ren 明确正面方向；当前 alignment 任务以 G0 为公理，不宣称起点已无前提得证<br>Direction stated by Ren; the current alignment task adopts G0 as an axiom, without claiming a premise-free proof of that starting point | [当前表述与范围 / Current statement and scope](../unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#shengsheng-as-the-positive-ethical-direction) |
| C21 | 共同形成本身的候选正面意义<br>Co-formation itself as a candidate favorable realization | 新关系使原本不能共同发生的生活及进一步形成条件共同实现时，整体形成一种新的实现方式；其正面意义不必先成为发起者收益。 | When new relations enable forms of living and conditions for further formation that could not previously occur together to be jointly realized, the whole gains a new mode of realization; its favorable significance need not first become a return to the initiator. | 本表核心哲学候选；不是由共同可行性直接证明的普遍伦理身份。<br>The central philosophical candidate; universal ethical identity is not proved merely by joint feasibility. | [所引原段 / Quoted passage](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-INTEGRATION.md#4-正面伦理推论得到更准确的对象但仍需要根据)<br>[完整正面论证 / Affirmative argument](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-ARGUMENT.md#5-the-strongest-affirmative-ethical-hypothesis) |
| C05 | 不以回报发起者为善的条件<br>Goodness need not return to the initiator | 实际的好与后续生成可以发生在别处或发起者退出之后；无需先证明它最终回报一个扩大的自我。 | Actual good and further generation can occur elsewhere or after the initiator leaves; they need not first be justified as benefits returning to an enlarged self. | 被辩护的整体伦理立场；普遍效力待证<br>Defended whole ethical position; universal authority open | [当前表述与范围 / Current statement and scope](../unified-2026-10-05/NONDUAL-METHOD.md#begin-within-generative-unfolding)<br>[相关论证 / Related argument](../unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#whole-appraisal-needs-no-additional-beneficiary) |
| C06 | 自身之善、休息与有限实现<br>Self-good, rest and finite realization | 自身照料、休息与有限完成属于整体中的真实实现，不必靠后续生产或额外外部收益才有地位。 | Self-care, rest and finite fulfillment are real realizations within the whole; they need not earn standing through later production or additional outward returns. | 明确保留的伦理范围；非生产义务<br>Explicitly retained ethical scope; no productivity duty | [当前表述与范围 / Current statement and scope](../unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#self-care-is-a-realization-within-the-whole)<br>[相关论证 / Related argument](../unified-2026-10-05/UNIFIED-ETHICAL-GROUNDING.md#renewal-and-practical-importance-do-not-exhaust-goodness) |

Joining probes further recover finite-fragment interfaces; the ternary relation determines unique unit roles, and complete finite traces recover their state interfaces. Global accounts remain eligible; capacity limits matching-unit sufficiency. The latest concrete summary-sufficiency proof derives an effective law and exact finite-history correspondence from the unchanged mechanism, without a separate Exact or homomorphism premise. The universal attempt characterizes arbitrary ternary-relation recovery: complete success answers and minimum states/edges still allow nonisomorphic mechanisms; deterministic reachable continuation-separated realizations recover uniqueness. The continuation derives finite-branch bisimulation from compound questions at shared conditions and reconstructs code histories lifting from every compatible native start; scoped structural uniqueness no longer requires determinism. Successor compactness further replaces finite branching, preserving structural correspondence and lifting whole infinite code runs; a genuinely infinitely branching positive model contrasts with equal finite-modal answers but different infinite realization. Joint questions further force step/joint correspondence under explicit correlated-pair compactness and lift finite nodewise plans; acquisition/release derives shared-support conflict. Complete isolated joint profiles can still lose designated-part aliasing, recovered by fixed-partner questions in the finite witness. Complete descendant support and sibling exclusion further connect arbitrary finite plans to distinct global requests and a common holding history; omission failures and a positive repair are checked. Every fixed n-ary success list can still miss an (n+1)-ary capacity restriction. Formation, withdrawal, acquisition and release have exact certificate-preservation conditions; every permitted finite history has a completing suffix in the same native law. Static lists can lose actual occupation, release/formation renew readiness, and an infinite permitted schedule can still never progress. All finite first-order identity, step and joint truths, even preserved by one coherent elementary map on every finite source tuple, do not force native isomorphism of wholly root-reachable worlds. This separate 54 (23+31) / 128 audit study has 105 empty and 23 propext-only lists. Probe access, state content and completion are explicit; identification with shared-capacity/concurrent conflicts remains open. New 14 theorems / 18 empty audits; 192 inherited maps unchanged (150 empty, 42 standard), independently reviewed. Existing controller/oxygen evidence is integrated without equating those cases with this construction or completing universal grounding. Overlapping imports are not added. This separate round adds 61 theorems / 135 audits (123 empty, 12 inherited propext; all new 61 empty). Existing effective-law results remain credited. Universal separation of native relations remains unwarranted; the scoped counterexample does not globally refute the conjecture. This round: 45 new theorems / 141 audits, 103 empty and 38 standard; new 19 empty and 26 standard, including 13 with classical choice. Finite branching, question coverage and reduction/separation are explicit; forward adequacy does not recover every actual past or prove universal explanatory scope. This round adds 36 theorems / 159 audits (102 empty, 57 standard; new 5 empty, 31 standard). Successor compactness is an explicit sufficient condition, not a universal law or finite-observation inference; whole continuation, actual history and general joint realization retain distinct scopes. This round adds 56 theorems / 183 audits (112 empty, 71 standard; new 6 empty, 50 standard). Pair compactness, question coverage and separation are explicit; nodewise joint plans do not establish arbitrary substitution or global concurrency, and universal uniqueness remains unproved. This round adds 37 theorems / 184 audits (115 empty, 69 standard; new 4 empty, 33 standard). Fixed access, persistent holding and operational correspondence are explicit; overapproximating footprints are sufficient certificates, and overlap alone is not failure. General dynamic joint realization and universal uniqueness remain unproved. This round adds 42 theorems / 211 audits (119 empty, 92 standard; new 6 empty, 36 standard, no new classical-choice dependency). Continuous readiness is stronger than eventual success after repair; atomic steps, full mediation and current-state access are explicit, and universal uniqueness remains unproved. This round adds 33 theorems / 180 audits (113 empty, 67 standard; new 2 empty and 31 standard, 13 with classical choice). The inference from first-order content to global coverage has a proved wall. | C14 | 生成对选择的递归影响<br>Recursive influence of generation on selection | 活动后果可改变后继形成与选择条件，贡献变体不必获益。重构、发育与聚合区分遗传群体和运输集合；细菌回复实验接通遗传切换与周期完成。谱系预测保留总体优势及局部失败。新增原始实证支持呼吸形成氧梯度并改变后续选择；结构形成史须接入发育迁移，常规解释获完整信用。 原始延时研究进一步记录界面小筏形成、下落和再形成；酸碱条件改变空间组织，须与持续膜结构和实际后代分别对应。 此前比较方向提出独立机制约束下的跨条件接续，以及重构和选择关系自身的改变。 新的24小时形成实测与常规模型进一步接通建立条件和留存占比；较低离散的优势取决于相对生长，反例保留。 已完成的演化组织说明进一步接通实际取样、继承发育状态与跨转移重建；组织改变后续取样结果，真实供给及后续修订均保留。 原始显微数据现核对真实尺度及逐位置拍摄时钟，混合培养两段采集的位置配置可接续；成像对应与培养物身份仍分别论证。 直接同等网络比较现证明重构机制具有精确的常规实现，包含依当前状态选择及失败行动。 Ren现明确要求聚焦理论：复用形成、重构、更新器及目的修订成果，比较其独立前提与额外解释，当时选定具体联结论学习结果的恢复。 预先固定视野的91帧现接成实测发育史；增长后的局部消失与轨迹的边界删失分别保留，观察结束不独自说明结构丧失。 新理论连接把后继重建能力接到完整回返路径的选择阈值及繁殖代价；直接外形延续可下降，第一代相同产出仍可掩盖相反的增长条件。 现已恢复单步Hebb方向，有限循环发育的后果响应由规律导出；tanh符号相容条件保持方向，并支持有限关系改变，反馈和选择关系改变可反转局部增益。 有限组装程序现从实际消耗及成功停止共同推出P列产出和重建比例；成功概率不同于后继比例，额外尝试可提高成功却消除增长优势。既有类型的增长因子接入相邻规则入侵条件。 新的共同历史由逐项非线性选择推出留存关系：同一网络从不完整线索重现两种格局，持续偏向一方造成旧格局失去重现能力，继续修订又能恢复；当前选择改善不保证保留旧能力。 实际传递组成与保留接触机会现导出局部成功率，后继自行重建成键；同一亲代、同平均成键率和接触消耗仍可因后继间机会分布不同而产生不同重建。不同种类传播体的响应不能无条件压成同一个回返系数。 后续同史构造把反复干扰造成的实际反应失败接到更新器维持与替换：由原生发育算出回报差别，保留旧关系与剩余支持，新规则实际改变后续更新并取得维持所需支持。 三阶段接续现证明条件性的有限周期回返，平衡混合时可用发育后响应的均值合并；成熟亲代的准备规律仍须实现。 组成生成与付费安放现实现指定亲代准备：同类生成使原先接触重建优势倒转，互补生成又改变排序；失败保留未用组成和支持，未完成指定返回不等于死亡。 参考接触现取得未知仿射通道的实际读数，形成的局部比较与抑制连接推出响应式更新，并在保留网络及支持记录中运行；校准后的通道变化可造成实际误报并阻止修复。 新参考比较现决定保留或修订已取得的读数，并实际恢复原网络更新；有限工作段之间可以改变通道。仿射通道零点相同、增益同向恰好保留全部符号判断，参数变化不独自等于能力丧失。 生长与断键现从实际连接图导出后继成员，接入共同材料准备；同组成及同边数可有不同共同传递，均匀断键与最老边先断使排序反转。新形成的替代维持关系还可使原有断键不再导致群体分裂。 实际逐步变化与噪声现推出取得读数的误差界，原生网络推出反应余量；工作段内通道可持续改变而在充分年龄窗口内保留实际修复及后续两类记忆。区间跨界保留不确定，可靠性取决于关系变化与辨别余量的共同条件。 | Consequences can change successor formation and selection without guaranteeing contributor advantage. Reconstruction, development and aggregation distinguish inherited clusters from transport groups; bacterial reversion links switching to cycle completion. Lineage forecasts retain aggregate gains and local failures. Primary evidence now links respiration-generated oxygen gradients to changed selection; structural development must enter transferable prediction. Conventional explanation receives full credit. Original time-lapse research adds recurring surface rafts and pH-sensitive spatial organization, requiring separate correspondences for persistent mat structure and actual descendants. Earlier comparative work proposed continuation across conditions constrained by independent mechanisms, followed by changes in reconstruction and selection relations. New 24-hour observations and a conventional model connect establishment to retained representation; lower dispersal gains only under the relative-growth condition, with an adverse contrast preserved. The completed evolutionary-organization account now connects actual sampling, inherited developmental state and reconstruction across transfers; formed organization changes later sampling, with actual provision and subsequent revision retained. Original microscopy now supplies checked scale and position-specific capture times, with continued position configuration across two rescue acquisitions; imaging correspondence and culture identity remain distinct. A direct matched-network comparison now establishes an exact conventional realization of the reconstruction mechanism, including shared state-dependent selectors and failed actions. Ren now directs a theoretical focus: reuse formation, reconstruction, updater and goal-revision results, compare their independent premises and added explanation, and selected recovery of a specific connectionist learning result. A predefined field now has a measured 91-frame developmental history; local disappearance after growth is distinguished from boundary censoring, so an observation ending does not alone establish structural loss. A new theoretical connection gives a full-cycle selection threshold and reproductive cost ceiling for inherited reconstruction; direct resemblance can fall, and identical first-generation outputs can conceal opposite growth conditions. The single-step Hebbian direction is now recovered. Finite recurrent sensitivity follows from the developmental law; a sign-compatible tanh regime preserves the direction through finite changes, while feedback and changed selective relations can reverse local gains. A finite assembly programme now derives P-column production and reconstruction jointly from paid attempts and stopping. Success probability differs from the descendant fraction, and added attempts can improve success while removing growth advantage. The resident factor enters an adjacent-rule invasion condition. A new common history derives retained relations from coordinate-wise nonlinear selection: the same network recalls two patterns from incomplete cues, continued emphasis on one destroys old recall, and further revision restores it. Current-score improvement does not guarantee preservation of an earlier capacity. Actual transmitted components and retained contact slots now determine local success, with each successor rebuilding its own bond. The same parent, mean encounter rate and cost can yield different reconstruction because opportunities are distributed differently across successors. Distinct seed responses cannot unconditionally be collapsed to one return coefficient. A subsequent common history connects actual response failures from repeated interference to updater maintenance and replacement: native development determines return differences, old relationships and remaining support persist, and the new rule governs later updates and earns renewal. A three-stage continuation proves conditional finite-cycle return and permits aggregation by the mean developed response under a balanced mixture; common mature-parent preparation still requires realization. Component production and paid routing now realize preparation of the specified parent: same-type production reverses the contact reconstruction ranking, while complementary production changes it again. Failed preparation retains components and work; missing the specified return does not establish death. Reference contacts now acquire actual readings of unknown affine channels. Formed local comparisons and inhibition derive responsive updating and operate in the retained network/support history; channel changes after calibration can cause false success and suppress repair. Fresh reference comparison now determines retention or revision of acquired readings and restores native updating across finite blocks with changed channels. Equal zero and same gain orientation exactly preserve the all-input affine sign interface; parameter change alone does not establish lost capacity. Growth and actual edge removal now derive successor membership from formed connections and join common material preparation. Equal composition and edge counts can yield different cotransmission; uniform and oldest-edge cuts reverse the ranking. A newly formed alternative retention route can also prevent a formerly separating cut from fragmenting the group. Actual successive changes and noise now imply acquired-reading error bounds, while native network responses imply a positive margin. Channels may keep changing within a block while actual repair and subsequent two-memory retention persist through a sufficient age window. Intervals crossing the boundary retain uncertainty; reliability depends jointly on relational change and discrimination margin. | | C28 | 尺度描述、误差与具体条件<br>Scale descriptions, error and particular conditions | 同一历史的变化描述可在明确误差界内组合，并支持逐项条件判断；精确总量仍可能遗漏具体损失，组织变化可使旧描述失效。取得证据后的付费、耗时修订可改变实际回应，但校准与有限支持仍可使其失败，固定回应也可成功。同一时限下的查询／直接回应比较进一步以共同可行回应的交集识别有关区别，保留查询过晚与支持不足，并修订选择条件。变化依存下还须校准记录有效期；刷新后仍可能失效，后续变化界给出有条件的修复。 此前保留旧读数的留出检验保留最大变化界被突破及迁移失败；该轮所试区间均宽于控制器软目标带，完整经验认证尚未成立。 后续局部历史模型改善18项留出预测比较，胜过同容量跨通道模型；三个窄百分位区间仍有实际失败。 接续充分性进一步要求活动后的每个观察类仍支持剩余时域的回应；一般递推等价于依记录分支的有限方案，保留眼前成功而后继冲突，以及取得区别／实际改变依存两种修复。 共同接续进一步证明：两份依各自记录分支的有限方案，在共同作用的局部投影覆盖与递推共同可实施条件下合成；共享消耗可使各自成功而共同后继阻塞。 有根据的相互接续从初始共同支持、一步共同可实施与相互保持推导递推方案相容；保留空许诺、空真保持、无启动能力及相位错开。 证据经取得到使用之间的变化传播后，须同时支持共同启动及两份局部方案的适用范围；已接回原合成定理，保留真实旧读数失效、原子保留修复及未受管理损失。 | Changing descriptions of one history compose under explicit error bounds and support indexed condition judgments; exact totals can still omit particular losses, and organizational change can defeat an earlier description. ## 四、学习、关切、良知与智慧怎样形成 / Formation of learning, concern, Liangzhi and wisdom

| 编号 / ID | 理论或命题 / Theory or proposition | 中文论断 | English formulation | 范围与状态 / Scope and status | 原文、证明与评审 / Sources, proofs and reviews |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| M01 | 依存、运作意义与调节<br>Dependency, operational significance and regulation | 组织所依存的关系改变其后续活动；当后果进入回应并改变支承条件时，调节便在同一过程中形成。 | Dependencies change what an organization can do next; regulation forms when consequences enter responses that alter those enabling relations. | 条件性机制与应用综合已接受；运作意义不等于体验或伦理价值。<br>Conditional mechanism and application synthesis accepted; operational significance is not experience or ethical value. | [依存、运作意义与调节 / Dependency, operational significance and regulation](../unified-2026-10-05/GENERATIVE-DEVELOPMENT.md) |
| M02 | 选择器及修订机制的形成<br>Formation of selectors and their revision mechanisms | 决定回应的机制本身可以形成、损耗、替换，并通过实际后果影响下一轮重建。 | Mechanisms selecting responses can themselves form, wear and be replaced, with actual consequences shaping subsequent reconstruction. | 有限条件构造与检查完成；备选规则、组装配方和提案律仍给定。<br>Finite conditional construction checked; alternative rules, assembly recipes and proposal laws remain supplied. | [选择器及修订机制的形成 / Formation of selectors and their revision mechanisms](../unified-2026-10-05/UPDATER-FORMATION.md)<br>[相关接续 / Related continuation](../unified-2026-10-05/SELECTOR-REVISION.md) |
| M03 | 后果学习、预测与行动<br>Consequence learning, prediction and action | 实际行动形成证据与留存记录；新证据可纠正预测并改变后续命令，但准确预测不保证行动有效。 | Actual action produces evidence and retained records; new evidence can correct prediction and change later commands, while accuracy alone does not ensure useful action. | 有限构造经 Lean 与独立数值重建检查；感知、解码及行动耦合的局部法则仍显式。<br>Finite construction checked in Lean and independent reconstruction; local sensing, decoding and action-coupling laws remain explicit. | [后果学习、预测与行动 / Consequence learning, prediction and action](../unified-2026-10-05/PREDICTION-ACTION.md) |
| M04 | 学习方式的后果性修订<br>Consequence-based revision of how learning occurs | 取得的诊断证据能够改变以后解释后果的机制，同时保留既有记录并传递修订结果。 | Acquired diagnostic evidence can change the mechanism interpreting later outcomes while retaining existing records and transmitting the revision. | 有限接口已检查；也能保留错误更新器，不保证演化改善学习。<br>Finite interface checked; it can also preserve a wrong updater and does not guarantee improving learning. | [学习方式的后果性修订 / Consequence-based revision of how learning occurs](../unified-2026-10-05/LEARNING-ROUTE-REVISION.md) |
| M05 | 后继学习与纠正的传递<br>Successor learning and transmission of correction | 实际形成的后继可以取得新证据、纠正继承的回应，并让下一代继承该纠正。 | An actually formed successor can acquire new evidence, correct an inherited response and transmit that correction to a further successor. | 有限谱系连接已检查；复制、运作与留存各需满足条件，错误回应也可被保存。<br>Finite lineage connection checked; copying, operation and retention have distinct conditions, and incorrect responses can also survive. | [后继学习与纠正的传递 / Successor learning and transmission of correction](../unified-2026-10-05/SUCCESSOR-LEARNING.md) |
| M06 | 对受影响关系的理解与纠正<br>Understanding and correcting recipient-directed action | 某处有效的回应可能在另一处失败；按实际关系区分记录，可以纠正后者而不覆盖前者的知识。 | A response effective in one relation can fail in another; context-specific records can correct the latter without overwriting the former knowledge. | 信息要求、有限实现与比较已检查；帮助改善可以没有私人资源增益。<br>Information requirements, finite realization and comparisons checked; improved help can yield no additional private resources. | [对受影响关系的理解与纠正 / Understanding and correcting recipient-directed action](../unified-2026-10-05/RECIPIENT-CORRECTION.md) |
| M07 | 证据、理由及修订的实际接续<br>Actual continuity of evidence, reasons and revision | 保留的证据经当前语境解释支持理由；撤回失效路径时，独立根据与实际重建条件必须保留。 | Retained evidence supports reasons through current contextual interpretation; withdrawing a defeated route must preserve independent grounds and actual reconstruction conditions. | 有限记录—理由—接续构造已验证；校准、事实到理由的关联及根理由仍需根据。<br>Finite record–reason–succession construction verified; calibration, fact-to-reason relevance and root reasons still need warrant. | [证据、理由及修订的实际接续 / Actual continuity of evidence, reasons and revision](../unified-2026-10-05/RECORDED-REASONS.md)<br>[相关接续 / Related continuation](../unified-2026-10-05/RECIPIENT-REASON-REVISION.md) |
| M08 | 取得的后果进入评价与选择<br>Acquired consequences entering evaluation and choice | 新取得的后果记录能够形成留存的评价影响，并改变为同一受影响者采取的实际行动。 | Newly acquired consequence records can form retained evaluative influence and change actual action for the same recipient. | 有限连接经 Lean 与独立重建接受；评价形成不独自证明普遍伦理有效性。<br>Finite connection accepted through Lean and independent reconstruction; formed evaluation does not itself prove universal ethical validity. | [取得的后果进入评价与选择 / Acquired consequences entering evaluation and choice](../unified-2026-10-05/EVALUATIVE-UPTAKE.md) |
| M09 | 评价影响、后继与新证据<br>Evaluative influence, succession and fresh evidence | 后继继承实际取得的评价影响，并以新证据修正手段、选择和实施行动。 | A successor inherits actually acquired evaluative influence and uses fresh evidence to revise means, choose and act. | 有限分阶段连接已接受；配方、校准、支持及伦理应用范围仍显式。<br>Finite staged connection accepted; recipes, calibration, support and ethical application remain explicit. | [评价影响、后继与新证据 / Evaluative influence, succession and fresh evidence](../unified-2026-10-05/EVALUATIVE-SUCCESSION.md) |
| M10 | 仁、感应、理解与实践的共同形成<br>Co-formation of ren, responsiveness, understanding and practice | 关切可以在共同活动中形成，并以实际受阻、缓解和展开纠正回应，而不只追求私人回报。 | Concern can form through shared activity and let actual obstruction, relief and development correct responses beyond private returns. | 构成解释、偏蔽复通及局部模型接口已发展；最小根基到仁的完整推导未完成。<br>Constitutive account, distortion/restoration analysis and local interfaces developed; the complete derivation of ren from the minimal root remains unfinished. | [仁、感应、理解与实践的共同形成 / Co-formation of ren, responsiveness, understanding and practice](../unified-2026-10-05/NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN.md) |
| M11 | 目的纠正与新继续方式<br>Purpose correction and new ways to continue | 达到终点不等于维持活动的全过程条件；若旧路径没有合格完成，仅筛选旧路径不能创造它。 | Reaching an endpoint does not ensure an activity's conditions held throughout; filtering old paths cannot create a qualified completion absent from them. | 一般条件定理已实际检查；给定活动的实现正确性不证明该活动正当。<br>General conditional theorems checked; correct realization of a specified activity does not prove its justification. | [目的纠正与新继续方式 / Purpose correction and new ways to continue](../unified-2026-10-05/NONINSTRUMENTAL-CONCERN.md) |
| M12 | 本然良知的生成演化与发展<br>Generative evolution and development of liangzhi | 先前活动改变发育、交往和再形成条件，使感应、照料与纠正能力能够传递和重建。 | Earlier activity changes developmental, social and reproductive conditions, allowing responsiveness, care and correction capacities to be transmitted and reconstructed. | 19项传递与17项发展声明已检查，有局部原始研究支持；完整良知历史与普遍可靠性未证。<br>Nineteen transmission and seventeen development declarations checked, with bounded primary evidence; a complete liangzhi history and universal reliability remain unproved. | [本然良知的生成演化与发展 / Generative evolution and development of liangzhi](../unified-2026-10-05/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md) |
| M13 | 良知的活动辨别与校准<br>Activity-sensitive discernment and calibration of liangzhi | 回应须辨明物品、手段及请求在实际共同活动中的作用，而非固定服从或固定物品评价。 | Responses must discern the role of objects, means and requests in actual shared activity rather than follow fixed obedience or object rankings. | 两项幼儿原始研究及旧证明对应经过限定评估；未证明完整发展史或伦理目的修订。<br>Two primary toddler studies and correspondence with earlier proofs assessed within bounds; no complete developmental history or ethical-purpose revision established. | [良知的活动辨别与校准 / Activity-sensitive discernment and calibration of liangzhi](../unified-2026-10-05/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md) |
| M14 | 关切的变通、交接与有限完成<br>Responsive concern, handoff and finite completion | 手段、目的、知情参与和既有承担的改变会改变回应；实际交接与合理结束也能参与接续。 | Changes in means, purposes, informed participation and undertakings change responses; actual handoff and justified endings can participate in continuation. | 20项交接、失败、恢复及有限结束声明已检查；依存不独自分配责任，成功交接不独自认证仁。 新 J3/J4 另行说明责任及分工；机制成功不自动赋予权限。<br>Twenty handoff, failure, recovery and finite-ending declarations checked; dependence alone allocates no duty, and successful handoff alone does not establish ren. New J3/J4 separately address responsibility and allocation; mechanism success does not confer authority. | [关切的变通、交接与有限完成 / Responsive concern, handoff and finite completion](../unified-2026-10-05/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION.md)<br>[实践责任与结束 / Practical responsibility and discharge](../unified-2026-10-05/PRACTICAL-JUDGMENT.md) |
| M15 | 智慧的协同修正与慧根<br>Coordinated correction in wisdom and huigen | 智慧协同因果理解、有根据的关切与可实行回应，并能够修正事实、排除规则和阻断纠正的权力。 本轮接续说明同一要求可有相反回应，前次成功也可实际改变下一步的条件。 新候选解释把共同生成约束的可追踪展开作为智慧推演能力的来源；理由通过真实实现关系及公开规范根据获得内容。 后续回应必要性检验表明：同一过程中的充分辨别可以不同，且未必有仍充分的共同最粗描述；共同根不预设共同最小表述。 目的评价接续定位成就指标遗漏的实际保护关系：相同旧、新成就表可有相反比较，固定的关系敏感规则亦能满足要求。 | Wisdom coordinates causal understanding, warranted concern and feasible response while revising facts, exclusion rules and power that blocks correction. A contextual continuation shows opposite responses under one requirement, including a reversal caused by the previous response’s success. A new candidate explains derivational productivity through traceable consequences of shared generative constraints; practical reasons acquire content through actual realization relations and explicit normative grounds. A subsequent response-necessity test shows distinct adequate descriptions of one process need not have an adequate common coarsening; a common root does not presuppose one least shared representation. A goal-evaluation continuation identifies omitted protection relations: identical old/new attainment profiles can have opposed practical frontiers, while a fixed relation-sensitive rule remains adequate. | 有界条件综合已接受；慧根的必要性、全部伦理内容与普遍智慧未证明。 形式支撑后续修复：原15项查询定理与1项辅助重证均为空依赖；连同实际自定义依赖，六模块63项审计全空。此修复不升级慧根或伦理的普遍必要性。 新11项定理／50项含依赖审计（44空、6标准），两组熟悉原典及不利案例按范围比较；未作盲法预测或证明伦理根据同一。 按 Ren 纠正，下一中心检验是固定共同前提、独立于目标格言给出推演，再比较最强常规解释。仅有哲学重构和有范围原典比较，无新增定理／实验；普遍发现能力与历史覆盖未证。 Ren 随后明确以证明“the root of wisdom”为本会话目标：须建立构成必要性并接通智慧的生成说明。共同受约束不能单独解释智慧；局部发表或例子不替代完整目标，根基仍未证。 此次另增9项 Lean 定理，连同50项继承共59项审计（44空、15标准）；新增8项仅propext，1项另含Quot.sound。固定一步技术过程、确定性回应与目标给定；不把可靠到达等同智慧，不宣称历史收敛。下一普遍连接是理由根据与目的修订，复用已完成机制及G0/J/E。 此次另增7项定理／11项审计，93项继承定理／165项审计分列；新增5项定理仅propext、1项仅Quot.sound、1项含二者，4项定义空报告。局部比较与全配置要求给定，未证明所有智慧必须接受J2；后续核对独立智慧范围及全部实质core，不重开已完成目的形成。<br>Bounded conditional synthesis accepted; huigen's necessity, full ethical content and universal wisdom remain unproved. Formal-support repair: the fifteen original inquiry theorems and one helper reproof have empty reports; their six-module logical/inquiry closure has 63 empty audits. This does not upgrade universal huigen or ethical necessity. Eleven new theorems / fifty import-inclusive audits (44 empty, 6 standard); two familiar primary-text comparisons retain adverse details. No blind prediction or identity of ethical grounds is claimed. Following Ren’s correction, the next central test fixes shared premises and derives judgments before matching maxims, against adequate conventional accounts. This is philosophical reconstruction and scoped primary comparison, with no new theorem or experiment; universal discovery and historical coverage remain unproved. Ren subsequently fixes proving “the root of wisdom” as this session’s objective: establish constitutive necessity and connect the formation of wisdom. Common constraint satisfaction alone does not explain wisdom. Local publication or examples do not replace the full target, which remains unproved. This continuation adds nine Lean theorems and fifty inherited audits, 59 overall (44 empty, 15 standard); eight new reports use only propext, one also Quot.sound. The one-step process, deterministic response and target are supplied. Guaranteed arrival is not identified with wisdom and historical convergence is not proved. The next universal connection concerns warranted ends and their revision, reusing completed mechanisms and G0/J/E. This continuation adds seven theorems / eleven audits, separately from 93 inherited theorems / 165 audits; five new proofs use propext only, one Quot.sound only, one both, with four empty definition reports. The local comparison and uniform adequacy requirement are supplied; J2 is not proved necessary for every wisdom. Further work concerns independent wisdom scope and the full substantive core, not reopening completed goal formation. | [智慧的协同修正与慧根 / Coordinated correction in wisdom and | M16 | 有效纠正的形成与战略脆弱性<br>Formation and strategic vulnerability of effective correction | 能够建设纠正能力与能够承受对方对其后续条件的破坏，是不同要求。 | Being able to build corrective capacity differs from being able to withstand attacks on its future conditions. | 条件战略构造与精确枚举已完成；保持历史验证范围，不升级为普遍制度结论。<br>Conditional strategic construction and exact enumeration completed; historical validation scope retained without a universal institutional conclusion. | [有效纠正的形成与战略脆弱性 / Formation and strategic vulnerability of effective correction](../unified-2026-10-05/FORMATION.md) |
| M17 | 关切、有效能力与更新的共同形成<br>Joint formation of concern, effective power and renewal | 学习、关切、查验、保护与后继重建可以在同一受支持的选择过程中相互接续。 | Learning, concern, inspection, protection and successor reconstruction can connect within one supported choice process. | 条件联合构造完成；廉价隔离、俘获和资源不足仍构成失败区，未证自然必然向善。<br>Conditional joint construction completed; cheap insulation, capture and inadequate resources remain failure regimes, with no inevitable favorable direction proved. | [关切、有效能力与更新的共同形成 / Joint formation of concern, effective power and renewal](../unified-2026-10-05/JOINT-FORMATION.md) |
| M18 | 可修订关切的联合实现<br>Joint realization of revisable concern | 理由修订、有效保护和资源支持的后继重建能够共存，但伦理过滤与战略均衡不能自动拼接。 | Reason revision, effective protection and supported successor reconstruction can coexist, but ethical filtering and strategic equilibrium do not compose automatically. | 条件联合过程与部分有限决策已建立；伦理锚点、审计可靠性和权限仍明确。<br>Conditional joint process and some finite decisions established; ethical anchors, audit soundness and authority remain explicit. | [可修订关切的联合实现 / Joint realization of revisable concern](../unified-2026-10-05/REVISABLE-CONCERN-SETTLEMENT.md) |
| M19 | 证据进入制度规则与实际补救<br>Evidence entering institutional rules and delivered remedy | 纠正个案不等于撤回失效的共同推断；纠正须沿实际依存传到规则、公共信息与补救。 | Correcting a case does not withdraw a defeated shared inference; correction must follow actual dependencies into rules, public information and remedy. | 有原始资料约束的历史应用与诊断契约；未估计设计的因果收益或完成普遍治理。<br>Primary-source-constrained historical application and diagnostic contract; no causal benefit estimate or complete general governance solution. | [证据进入制度规则与实际补救 / Evidence entering institutional rules and delivered remedy](../unified-2026-10-05/EVIDENCE-AND-INSTITUTIONAL-CORRECTION.md) |
| M20 | 生成性对齐：当前首要方向之一<br>Generative alignment: a current primary direction | 以生生为共同根据评价目标、评价者与选择规则；方向论证及相连目标取得、判断、实际修订与有界改革现已成为完成成果，更强理论按明确范围继续。 后续接通改革自身改变实际负担及报告路线、直接取得与保留新目的的修复。 理论接续区分发现已有根据、形成新生活与理由、修订评价承诺；学习后的赞同不独自认证形成过程。 在已完成的伦理学习基础上，新增比较接口辨明理由内容扩展后旧判断的传递、不可比及有前提的反转。 评价扩展进一步区分共同改善与各应用分别否定；旧内容保持不要求旧评价永久保持。 理论接续给出生活实现、支承与进一步形成的具体根据；共同生活不能以组织规模或产出代替。 后天形成的支承可以因其在基本生活中的实际作用取得保护；生成的评价须包括其形成的后续支持要求。 形成出的生活、承受和参与可以改变原服务角色的伦理根据；考虑、责任与支配权限分列。 新增取得记录到路线必要性分类的连接：忠实读取与选项覆盖支持有范围的判断，一个真实替代即可推翻不可替代性；缩小有根据解释与扩大可行选项分列。 自我评估的选择现在有精确覆盖条件：允许有根据的压缩，区分当前菜单与任意菜单；有限成功不能独自担保新范围，全域结构加辨别记录提供正面出路。 理论接续说明理解怎样实际参与目的和准备：关切可以与报酬及其他正当动机并存，其形成条件也须接受同一规范评价。 BC1 在同等基本救助的既定范围内支持保全更多生活；BC2 的一对一说明以可避免的实际毁损给出比较理由，区分境况、保护与时机，并保留机会争议。 形成中的评估区分当前能力、过去能力及形成方法。Ren 后续指出，历史区别无法恢复，不阻止以未来事件取得同一内容的新根据或修订理解；情境把握与风险估计可支持继续生成。 实际调查现接入描述与读取的共同修订：同一事件在某项估计中排除，不抹去其他有根据的表达意义；纠正归属与保留参与、支持可以属于同一回应。 关切发展接续具体化真实根据保留、改变及新形成时的双向纠正，并将学习中取得的解释纳入形成账户；五条候选路线保留各自正面作用。 按后续指示完成路线比较：以已有局部关切取得新关系内容并重组目的为理论主线，配套形成史判别，再选择机制研究；认识进步与实际新理由不以价值权重增加为定义。 LC 接通数量与程度共同改善的有限损失比较理由：实际损失的有根据匹配及额外保护可以共同支持方向；重复描述不增加生活，共同依存须进入损失判断。 情境风险接续区分个别改善与共同失效；精确有限界限允许部分把握和正风险，支持关系修订可解除共同中断，同时保留分散损失。 有限合并接续区分抵偿限制与理由删除：最强层过滤可在增加实际基本保护后翻转选择；固定比较与局部胜出拼接的条件分别公开。 关切内容形成现有一条具体候选：已有局部关切使粗略成功与实际回应的分离取得实践意义，关系辨别通过计划修订改变目的；实际新生活及对它的动机取得分列。 LR 为同等多救的充分优先提出局部稳定承诺，使一部分稍不等的基本损失有正面比较；新增共同保护另可逐次保留旧保护，增加原先冲突的实现。 同等基本风险分担RS现有正面理由：完整损失分布相当时，减轻无根据的暴露集中；同频率选择可产生不同真实风险，新信息也能支持进一步改规则。 现实市场与互联网为 enshittification 的机制追踪提供事件：以既有外部性解释为起点，查优势怎样改变未来选择及纠正条件，不将增长与损失并存当作新发现。 机会修订接续表明：最终保护的完整联合分布相同仍可能撤销正当安顿；选择规则须保留仍有效的历史要求，并说明真实纠正的根据。 陌生与无回报的关切扩展现有两段条件性说明：辨明对方在自身参与之外的生活，再取得陌生处境中的相关根据；共同史是形成入口，不是全部关切的边界。 CP为不等且未被充分排除的基本请求提出真实正机会要求，将共同分配权限与基本回应关系相连；它是新增且可争辩的进入承诺。 陌生生活辨认现接通具体实现、支撑关系、可修订证据与有限回应；观察与分类方式也可因遗漏的生活根据而修订。 共同接口史进一步支持直接经营控制与有偿影响的区分：反馈和替代的出现也可能进入维持优势的规则调整，改革需追踪实际控制关系。 RSL进一步提出有根据分担判断的有限延续；中间混合方案降低极端取舍，修订须携带理由及其整体适用范围。 风险接续新增 NP 候选：相同基本要求下，微小暴露差距不自动获得固定额外优先；在真实细分可行范围内，有条件地反对有损的精确均衡。 初次共同接管的机会限制现可连接到J3：先明确仍有效承担所保护的具体支持，再检验新安排是否兑现；名义正机会不独自完成接管任务。 选择压力下的关切现有个人补救—依存取得—共同支持—实际评定—接替的条件性形成说明；旧分数、真实损失及行动条件分别辨明。 退化边界续研区分伦理损失、返回机制与状态域：原市场模型中纠正余量精确决定统一门槛保护及指定低区证书的存在，同参数仍可保留良性域。 取得权力后的关切现有根据—有限授权—有内容的接替—可实行复核的条件性说明；充分比较可要求改变或结束原掌权方式。 随后新增实际Lean连接：具体强维护的逐情形覆盖导出同律保护质量下界；全非负自然数权重的保持刻画覆盖，一项实际法则同成功率仍可失履行。 情境把握新增有条件的估计—保留误差界—行动根据连接：变化中可收敛或有界追踪，具有正比较余量时可在有限阶段取得充分认证。 纠正入口现有由共同准备、有限授权与实际承接形成的有限候选；正当回应可跨过不愿纠正的协调者，动机取得与回应实现分别说明。 风险条件接续要求同时交代当前支持的概率对应与仍有效历史；固定旧分数或不断重算分数都不足以完成规则对齐。 指定维护新增实际执行组合：已有观察分支选择器取得真实读数并实施，经明确任务效果对应导出履行及质量下界；有限预算中旧维护仍可行而完整扩展失败，增资后付费读取或实际并行均能扩展保护。 取得的行动响应可进入预测，补偿持续变化；精度须与行动幅度共同判断，有限相关条件可联合达到足够认证，扩展前沿仍可有新未明关系。 §§67–74现接通新生活根据进入有范围共同复核，并区分履行修订、新影响责任、控制收缩及正当新增任务；认识范围扩大不预定机构扩权。 局部吸引续研已把原市场模型的双吸引与内部不稳定接回同一历史的实际支持：选择组织能够吸收扰动，同时耗尽必要支持；高端保护与低端有限失效分列。 | Appraise ends, evaluators and selection rules through shared generative grounds; the direction argument and connected acquisition, judgment, operative revision and bounded reform are now completed foundations for the broader programme. A further joined case connects reform-induced burdens and reporting changes to acquired correction and repair preserving the new goal. The theoretical continuation distinguishes discovering existing grounds, forming new living relations and reasons, and revising normative commitments; later endorsement alone does not authorize the formation process. Building on the completed ethical-learning account, a new comparison interface delimits preservation, incomparability and conditional reversal after warranted content extension. Evaluative extension further separates a common improvement from defeat in each live application; retained old content need not preserve every old verdict. The continuation articulates living realization, support and further formation as particular grounds; organizational scale and output cannot substitute for the shared living being assessed. Acquired support can warrant basic protection through its actual role in living; appraisal of formation includes the subsequent support requirements it creates. Formed living, undergoing and participation can change the grounds of an original service role; consideration, responsibility and governing authority remain distinct. A further connection derives scoped route classification from faithful acquired records and covered options; one genuine alternative defeats indispensability. Narrowing supported interpretations and expanding eligible options are distinct changes. Self-review selection now has exact certificate-coverage conditions: warranted compression is allowed, current-menu and every-menu adequacy differ, and finite success alone cannot certify a new scope. Whole-domain structure plus discriminating records supplies a positive route. The conceptual continuation explains how understanding can participate in purposes and preparation: concern can coexist with pay and other legitimate motives, while its formative conditions face the same normative appraisal. BC1 favors more extensive equivalent basic rescue in its established scope. The one-to-one account of BC2 supplies a reason based on preventable impairment, distinguishing baseline, protection and timing while retaining fair-chance disputes. Formative assessment distinguishes current capacity, earlier capacity and the method of formation. Ren’s correction clarifies that unavailable history need not prevent fresh grounds for the same content or revised understanding through future events; situational awareness and risk estimates can support continued generativity. Actual inquiry now connects joint description-and-reading revision to practice: excluding an event from one estimate need not erase its independently warranted expressive significance. Corrected attribution and continued participation/support can belong to one response. The developmental continuation specifies bidirectional correction as living grounds persist, change or form, and includes acquired interpretation during learning; five candidate routes retain their positive roles. A subsequent route assessment prioritizes acquired relational content and purpose revision within partial concern, with discriminating formation histories and conditional mechanistic investigation; epistemic progress and new particular reasons are not defined by increased valuation weights. LC supplies a limited comparative reason where number and gravity jointly improve: warranted matching and additional actual protection can support a direction together. Duplicate descriptions do not multiply living; shared dependence must inform loss assessment. Situated risk now distinguishes marginal improvement from shared failure. Exact finite bounds permit partial awareness and nonzero risk; revising support can remove joint interruption while retaining dispersed losses. The aggregation continuation distinguishes limited counterbalancing from deletion of reasons: a strongest-tier filter reverses under added actual protection; fixed-comparison and local-composition premises are separately exposed. A concrete concern-content candidate links a coarse success conception, meaningful-response mismatch, acquired relational distinctions and operative purpose revision; actual new shared goods and their motivational uptake remain distinct. LR adds a local stability commitment to BC1’s sufficient priority, supporting some slightly unequal basic-loss comparisons. Separately, newly feasible joint protection can preserve old protection on each realization and add previously conflicting protection. RS now supplies a defended reason to share comparable basic risk while preserving the complete loss profile. Equal selection frequencies can conceal different exposure; new information can support further rule revision. Existing market and internet histories provide events for tracing enshittification: start with established externality explanations and investigate how advantage changes future selection and correction, rather than treating growth alongside loss as a new finding. The chance-revision continuation shows that even equal full terminal joint laws can conceal replacement of a warranted settlement; selectors must preserve live historical requirements and explain genuine correction. A conditional extension account now distinguishes continuation beyond one's participation from acquired grounds in unfamiliar lives; shared history can form concern without delimiting every ground it can address. CP proposes actual positive opportunity for unequal basic requests not adequately excluded, grounding a limit on common allocative authority in the response relationship; it is an additional contestable admission commitment. An unfamiliar-life recognition account now connects particular realization, support, revisable evidence and bounded response; omitted living grounds can also warrant revision of observation and classification. A shared-interface history supports distinguishing direct operational control from purchased influence: feedback and alternatives can enter rule changes that preserve advantage, so reforms must track actual control relations. RSL proposes limited retention of a warranted sharing judgment. Intermediate mixtures refine the choice; revision must carry its grounds and their whole-comparison scope. Risk continuation adds NP: tiny exposure differences do not automatically confer a fixed extra priority among matched basic claims. It conditionally rejects costly exact equality when sufficiently fine departures are feasible. Initial common takeover constraints are now connected to J3: specify the concrete support still due, then test whether the replacement fulfills it. Nominal positive opportunity alone does not discharge the task. A conditional account now connects personal compensation, acquired dependencies, shared support, consequential assessment and succession under selection pressure, distinguishing score loss, living loss and conditions for action. The degeneration extension separates ethical loss, returning selection and state domains: an exact correction margin governs uniform floor protection and existence of the specified low-region certificates, while a favorable region can coexist. A conditional account connects living grounds, bounded authorization, meaningful succession and effective review; the inherited sufficient comparison can require changing or ending incumbent control. A subsequent checked Lean bridge derives common-law support mass bounds from pointwise maintenance; preservation for all nonnegative natural weights characterizes coverage, while equal success under one actual law can still miss the undertaking. Situated awareness now has a conditional estimation–retained uncertainty–decision evidence connection: convergence or bounded tracking through change, with eventual certification under a strict comparison margin. A finite candidate forms corrective access through cooperative preparation, bounded delegation and actual succession; warranted response can pass an unwilling coordinator, with motive acquisition and performance kept distinct. Risk conditioning requires both current-support probability correspondence and warranted continuing history; neither freezing nor repeatedly recalculating a score completes rule alignment. Specified maintenance now has an actual-execution composition: the existing contingent selector acquires an observation and responds, with explicit task-effect correspondence yielding fulfillment and a mass bound. Old maintenance remains feasible when full expansion fails; funded sensing or real parallel provision realizes the extension. Acquired action response can enter prediction and compensate sustained change. Precision must be assessed with action magnitude; a finite relevant family can jointly reach adequate certification while an expanding frontier retains new unknowns. Sections 67–74 connect acquired living grounds to scoped shared review, distinguishing corrected fulfillment, accountable new effects, contraction and justified new undertakings. Wider understanding need not expand institutional power. The attraction continuation connects two local attractors and interior instability to actual support in the same market history: a selection organization can absorb perturbations while exhausting essential support; high-side protection and low-side finite failure remain distinct. | 当前首要方向之一；方向论证及本轮相连机制均已独立验收，不再作为待做接口。新相连结果：27新定理／84新声明审计、46继承审计，13历史／221完整状态；规范采用、对应、权限、有限资金与现实部署范围分明。 Lean 定理／52项声明审计，58项导入定理／66项审计分列；有理参数族平衡分类、指定截面的局部趋近和有限脱离、逐步对应及实际失效守卫已查。无完整吸引域、全部参数分岔、实数推广、经验校准或独立验收。<br>A current primary direction. | M21 | 知识与生活：相连的哲学候选<br>Knowing and living: connected philosophical proposal | 认识、纠正与模型修订构成相连候选。G0下意识分化生的意义，新的生也可有正面意义且不自动产生特定义务。保存已有生活能够要求实际更新；更强争点是超出既有请求与保存目标的新实现。整体改名不能隐去毁损，拒绝福利性创造理由不等于否定全部生成根据。 有条件的照护不独自穷尽生成理由，排除其他正面根据须另有辩护。 完整历史中的混合改善与正当重新分配可导出新的生的正面比较；先后评价须有明确传递。 完整排序不是基本正面意义的新增门槛；新生的正面理由与具体行动要求分别说明。 生的意义不以调节成功或自足为门槛；依存中的生活与局部成功的整体后果分别保留。 意识比较完成两问：福利限制不独自穷尽伦理意义，感觉改变须重评，不能自动保留或抹去全部价值。 探究目的检验区分正确性、探究理由与更广生活根据；理解可以本身值得实现，但使用科学不独自驳倒对 G0 的范围限制。 共同生活根据检验说明 G0 与 J1/J4 能在无反向损失且正当可行时支持非意识生活的继续；条件性选择差异不反过来证明公理。 理由权威检验即使承认客观实践理由，仍须解释其具体内容为何涵盖 G0 的生的范围；保留目的修订及可错纠正的正面账户。 具体理由比较完成照护、理解、正当承担及进一步生的接通；共同根据不要求价值独占，关系中的好可以本身值得实现。 福利边界检验允许意识福利与更广生的基本意义并存；比较优先不抹去被舍弃的真实好，争议转向福利是否穷尽全部基本伦理根据。 福利排他论比较完成：实际的好不等于新增改善或义务；无意识生的概念比较定位根据分歧，反驳一种排他论不独自证明G0及全部附加承诺的唯一必然。 繁盛肯定论证进一步区分生物表现、基本意义与整体选择：完整繁盛不是生的意义门槛，繁殖和典型性不是整体好坏的充分判准。 相容性纠正恢复原目标：不要求唯一表述或实际赞同；明确排除生的基本意义才构成相应冲突。 实践权威检验复用已完成责任原则：一时意愿改变不独自解除正当承担；认识理由的权威不自动完成实践权威。 | Knowing, correction and model revision form a connected account. Under G0, consciousness differentiates living significance and new living can count positively without assigning a specific duty. Preservation can require renewal; the sharper dispute concerns new realization beyond incumbent claims and preservation targets. Redescription cannot erase destruction, and rejecting a welfare-based creation reason does not reject every generative ground. Conditional care does not independently exhaust formation reasons; excluding a further favorable ground needs its own defense. Whole-history mixed improvement and warranted reallocation yield favorable formation; stage-relative judgments need explicit transport. A complete ordering is not an additional gate for basic positive significance; favorable formation reasons and practical requirements retain distinct grounds. Living significance is not awarded for regulatory success or self-sufficiency; dependent living and the whole consequences of local success remain in view. The consciousness comparison closes the two questions: a welfare restriction alone does not exhaust ethical significance, and changed feeling requires reassessment without automatic retention or erasure of all value. The inquiry-purpose test distinguishes correctness, reasons to inquire and wider living grounds; understanding can itself be worth realizing, but using science alone does not refute a restriction of G0. The common-living-ground test shows how G0/J1/J4 can favor nonconscious living continuation under no-loss and legitimate-feasibility conditions; that conditional difference does not prove the axiom. The reason-authority test grants objective practical reasons while requiring an explanation of why their content reaches G0 living scope; purpose revision and fallible correction retain their positive roles. The completed reason-content comparison connects care, understanding, legitimate undertakings and further living; a common ground need not exhaust all value, and relational goods can matter for their own sake. The welfare-boundary test permits conscious welfare alongside wider basic living significance; comparative priority preserves foregone good, and the dispute concerns whether welfare exhausts basic ethical grounds. The welfare-exhaustion comparison distinguishes realized good, marginal improvement and duty; nonconscious living locates a substantive ground dispute, while rejecting one restriction does not uniquely establish G0 or every additional commitment. The affirmative flourishing test distinguishes biological performance, basic significance and whole choice: complete flourishing is not an admission threshold, and reproduction or typicality does not independently determine whole goodness. The restored target requires compatibility, without demanding a unique formulation or actual assent; explicit exclusion of basic living significance supplies the corresponding conflict. Maintenance has a real cost, and filtering requires recomputed continuation values. Finite-horizon composition retains prefix access and attains the constrained optimum. A finite-weight counterexample shows that a local repair must be executable from available observations: exact optimality can retain a 1/16 false-assertion risk. Chosen sensing can invalidate an initially sufficient risk floor; guarantees must cover later observations and preserve usable records and command delivery. A two-channel protocol acquires and renews decoding records while retaining the old route until the new one is ready; matching versions alone do not establish record soundness. Terminal correction is realizable exactly when each observation admits a common successful command; conflicting hidden decoders obstruct guarantees, while a protected use interval supplies a conditional repair. Complementary observations and separately addressed actions across systems can change that boundary; a shared whole permits useful trials, but local success alone does not justify joint intervention. An exact possibility interface does not alone preserve observable correction; aggregate probes and relational repair can work, while preserving the composed relation allows continuing internal change. Followup: 31 theorems / 54 audits, including randomized-policy optimality, executable repair bounds and a risk-floor condition. Explicit paper correspondence and author counterreview yield a concrete contribution for external scrutiny; independent acceptance, absolute priority and learned-AI advantage remain open. | F01 | 过程核心、谓词与路径组合<br>Process core predication and composition | 给定变化关系后，有限历史通过实际共同中间配置组合；谓词可陈述保持性、可能性及全称终点性质，忠实抽象有明确局部条件。 生成逻辑首轮在左单位与原生上下文相容条件下，证明资源演算的收缩可容许当且仅当实际复制可达；有限供给有精确付费替代界限。 新的有限逐前缀继续谓词接入同一核心：每个请求步骤可继续，全部完成终点满足目标；有相反回应与成功后须调整的机制见证。 第二轮从原生占用规律推出局部历史允许的固定上下文精确判据；单相关支承位摘要保留所选工作负载的执行与整体事件步数预算。 第三轮证明：当前接入相同，允许改接范围内占用一致，当且仅当全部允许有限历史可行性一致。 第四轮将有来源保证的记录与逐位置核对接到同一原生取得过程；任意有限执行投影合法，并保留取得／使用预算。 无预设元逻辑续研提出同一规则的构成性根据候选：同一根据在同一认可要求下直接承接；完整奠基仍待完成。 接续区分由引入／使用构成的共同指向与经验再辨认；给定有限语法中，无来源、无周围假设的原子型 A → A 项必定返回所给根据。 | Given a transition relation, finite histories compose through a shared reached configuration; predicates express invariance, possibility and universal endpoint properties, with explicit local conditions for faithful abstraction. A finite prefix-sensitive obligation extends the same core: every requested step can continue and all completed endpoints meet the target, with mechanisms for opposite responses and adjustment after success. A continuation derives the exact frozen-context criterion from native occupancy laws; a relevant-slot summary preserves selected execution and merged-state event-count budgets. A third round proves that, at equal current routes, agreement on permitted-slot occupancy is equivalent to agreement on every allowed finite-word feasibility judgment. A fourth round connects maintained records and per-slot validation to the same native acquisition process, proving finite-trace projection and separate acquisition/use accounting. Direct metalogical inquiry adds a constitutive identity-rule candidate: re-presenting the same ground under the same assessment requirement; full grounding remains unfinished. A continuation distinguishes constituted binding from independent reidentification: every closed source-free atomic endomorphism in the supplied finite language returns its argument. | 逻辑、配置／事件域及变化关系明确给定，未由此导出全部规律或伦理方向。完整说明附六模块61项空依赖审计，其中核心18项、新教学定理12项；可能性、无执行时的空真与实际进展分别保留。旧回执仍可查。 新38项定理／65项总审计（51空），含顺序复用不保证重叠、上下文干扰与完整补给包不可复制的反例。形成的是即时就绪；任意有限准备已计入可达性。未导出元逻辑或确立前沿新颖性；第一轮提出的上下文与摘要目标已由本次有范围结果接续。 新11项定理继承39项审计；50项中44空、6仅标准依赖。终点正确可遗漏中断；实际调度、物理时间与目标伦理根据另列。 新37项定理／220项闭包审计（124空）；新证明无经典选择。初始完整相容性、局部历史与固定占用条件明示；总量摘要失败，单支承摘要不能认证遗漏位置的相容性。整体预算不等于保持其他占用的准备，步数不等于真实时间。Iris/Leaf已有先例，仍属完成的整合而非新颖性证明。 新24项定理／42项总审计（30空）；原生三步历史产生旧工作负载等价、扩展范围可区分的配置。旧摘要加目的地不足以总能更新摘要。允许范围、原子性和身份给定；不禁止无损编码，未解决新读数取得或确立前沿新颖性。已完成交错研究直接复用。 新28项定理／70项闭包审计（38空）；新证明8空、20仅标准依赖，无经典选择。无关改变可继续；指定n次失效后读取／使用需n+2单位，任意有限预算有耗尽见证。原子可信读取、全部写入版本维护及原子核对／使用给定；此局部连接已完成，现实对应及新颖性未证。 新11项形式审计均为空命名公理报告，仍使用 Lean 元理论。混合认可标准可不反身，反身性不独自保证接续；Curry 型固定点控制限制任意自我担保。实查不代替内容／根据辨认的哲学说明，既有无前提不可能性未证，亦未宣称完成或新逻辑。 新7项定理／21项含继承声明审计均空；继承14项不重计为新。关系保持由语法归纳得到；额外上下文或来源各有替代输入的见证。use解释、语法及元理论仍给定，实际绑定与假设代入的最终根据未证；非全体复合类型或任意程序的唯一性，不宣称新颖性。<br>Logic, configuration/event domains and dynamics remain explicit inputs; this does not derive every law or ethical direction. The exposition checks six modules with 61 empty dependency audits, including 18 core audits and 12 new pedagogical theorems; possibility, vacuity and actual progress remain distinct. The earlier receipt is retained. New pilot: 38 theorems / 65 total audits (51 empty), retaining serial/overlap, contextual interference and funded-package duplication boundaries. Immediate readiness forms; unrestricted reachability already includes preparation. Metalogic and novelty are not established; the first-round context/abstraction target is continued by the scoped results below. Eleven new theorems plus 39 inherited audits; 44 of 50 reports empty, six standard only. Correct endpoints can omit interruption; scheduling, physical time and ethical target warrant remain separate. Continuation: 37 new theorems / 220 closure audits (124 empty), no new choice dependency. Full initial compatibility, a valid local history and a frozen frame are explicit. Counts lose readiness; the selected summary loses hidden-slot compatibility. Merged-state budgets do not imply preparation preserving held occupancy, and steps are not physical time. Iris/Leaf precedents remain credited; the scoped integration is complete, without a novelty claim. Reconfiguration: 24 new theorems / 42 total audits (30 empty). A native three-event history witnesses old-workload equivalence and separation under wider access; old active-view plus destination cannot always determine the new view. Permission scope, identities and atomicity remain supplied. Lossless encodings remain possible; observation acquisition and novelty are not established. Completed interleaving work is reused. Acquired-use continuation: 28 new theorems / 70 closure audits (38 empty); new reports are 8 empty and 20 standard only, with no choice. Unrelated change is allowed; n specified invalidation cycles then read/use cost n+2 units, and every finite budget has an exhaustion witness. Coherent atomic reads, full writer revision maintenance and atomic validate/use remain supplied. This local bridge is complete; realization and novelty are not established. Eleven new formal audits have empty named-axiom reports but retain Lean's metatheory. Mixed standards can fail reflexivity; reflexivity alone does not ensure composition. A Curry fixed-point control limits unrestricted self-certification. Formal checks do not establish philosophical recognition of content/ground; neither impossibility, completed presuppositionless grounding nor a new logic is claimed. Seven new theorems / 21 total declaration audits are empty, including 14 inherited audits. Relation preservation follows by syntax induction; ambient and introduced grounds give controls. Context-use interpretation, syntax and metatheory remain supplied; actual binding and hypothetical-substitution warrant remain open. | F20 | 尺度近似与条件传递的形式检查<br>Formal checks of scale approximation and condition transport | 有限时域误差递推、两尺度组合、指标校准和条件传递经过实查，并保留耦合变化、误差累积及总量丢失局部条件的反例。后续检查实际证据取得、描述修订、误差余量与回应，保留耗时、读数偏差、工作耗尽及支持不足的失败和固定回应成功。同一时限下的查询／直接回应比较进一步以共同可行回应的交集识别有关区别，保留查询过晚与支持不足，并修订选择条件。变化依存下还须校准记录有效期；刷新后仍可能失效，后续变化界给出有条件的修复。 此前保留旧读数的留出检验保留最大变化界被突破及迁移失败；该轮所试区间均宽于控制器软目标带，完整经验认证尚未成立。 后续局部历史模型改善18项留出预测比较，胜过同容量跨通道模型；三个窄百分位区间仍有实际失败。 接续充分性进一步要求活动后的每个观察类仍支持剩余时域的回应；一般递推等价于依记录分支的有限方案，保留眼前成功而后继冲突，以及取得区别／实际改变依存两种修复。 共同接续进一步证明：两份依各自记录分支的有限方案，在共同作用的局部投影覆盖与递推共同可实施条件下合成；共享消耗可使各自成功而共同后继阻塞。 有根据的相互接续从初始共同支持、一步共同可实施与相互保持推导递推方案相容；保留空许诺、空真保持、无启动能力及相位错开。 证据经取得到使用之间的变化传播后，须同时支持共同启动及两份局部方案的适用范围；已接回原合成定理，保留真实旧读数失效、原子保留修复及未受管理损失。 | Checked finite-horizon error propagation, two-scale composition, readout calibration and condition transport retain counterexamples for changed coupling, accumulated error and local conditions lost by exact totals. Continuation checks actual acquisition, revision, error margins and response, retaining delay, sensor bias, exhausted work, insufficient support and a successful fixed response. Common-horizon inquiry/immediate-response comparison identifies consequential distinctions through common admissible actions, retaining late inquiry and inadequate support and repairing the selection guard. Changed dependencies require calibrated temporal validity; a fresh reading can still fail, and bounded subsequent change supports a conditional repair. Earlier retained-reading tests retain maximum-envelope violations and transfer failures; every interval in that round exceeds the controller’s soft target width, leaving empirical certification incomplete. A subsequent local-history model improves 18 held-out comparisons and beats an equal-size coupled model; three narrow percentile intervals retain actual misses. Continuation adequacy further requires each post-activity observation class to support the remaining horizon; the recursion is equivalent to a finite observation-dependent plan, retaining later conflict and repairs through information or actual dependency change. Joint continuation composes two finite local observation-dependent plans under faithful projection of joint effects and recursive joint executability; shared consumption can block joint successors despite separate success. Seeded mutual continuation derives recursive plan compatibility from initial joint support, one-step joint availability and mutual preservation, retaining empty promises, vacuous preservation, absent ignition capacity and phase mismatch. Evidence propagated through acquisition-to-use changes must certify both the shared seed and local-plan domains; the existing composition theorem is reused, with stale truthful records, atomic-reservation repair and unmediated loss retained. | | F21 | 组织、选择与尺度对应的形式检查<br>Formal checks of organization, choice and scale correspondence | 所选行动影响之差进入有限时域误差界；阈值余量足以保持指定选择，近阈值反例及共享支持被他者消耗后的计划失败均经过实查。 新构造证明实际描述组合、保持历史与区域差异的必要性，并接通有支持的选择与后续接触。 | Differences in selected action effects enter the finite-horizon error bound; threshold margins suffice to preserve specified choices, with checked near-threshold counterexamples and failed retained plans after another participant consumes shared support. The new construction proves actual view composition and the consequential role of retained history and regional differences, connecting supported selection to subsequent contact. | 旧14项定理／63项继承审计及新26项定理／77项继承审计分别保留。标准逻辑，无自定义公理；规律、路线、保持、费用、时序与对应明确给定。后续经验校准、分子证据及有限生态回返不增加定理，不从拟合残差推出普遍误差界，也不完成整个生物闭环。<br>Earlier 14 theorems / 63 inherited audits and new 26 theorems / 77 inherited audits remain separate. | F22 | 关系恢复、组合唯一性与接口重建<br>Relation recovery, composition uniqueness and reconstructed interfaces | 继承校准、结构对手、组合唯一性及实际展开的接合／替换、分支继续与同规律错误反例；另增空历史探针、局部单位唯一性与关系同构下保持、完整接续充要条件和整个状态接口恢复。容量及内部序列丢失反例保留。 最新从原有机制直接证明摘要充分性，推出有效规律与全部有限历史精确对应，不另给Exact或同态前提。 普遍化检验给出任意三元关系恢复的充要条件；完整成功答案与最少状态／边仍允许不同构机制，确定且可达、后续可区分时恢复唯一对应。 后续以共同条件下的复合接续问题推出有限分支双模拟，并由准确约简答案构造可从每个相容原生起点提升的编码规律；结构唯一性不再要求确定性。 再以后继紧致性放宽有限分支，取得结构对应及完整无限编码路径提升；有无限多个可区分后继的正例，也有全部有限复合答案相同而无限持续不同的反例。 共同问题再在明示成对紧致性下迫出步骤／接合对应并提升有限逐节点方案；实际占用／释放推出共享冲突。全部孤立共同问题仍可能遗漏指定部分的共享身份，固定搭配问题在有限实例中恢复。 再以完整下层支持覆盖及同级独占支持分离，将任意有限方案接到全局无重复与一次共同占用历史；遗漏关系的失败及修复正例均检查。每个固定 n 阶成功清单仍可漏掉 n+1 阶共享容量限制。 变化中的形成、撤去、占用与释放具有精确证书保持条件；任意允许有限历史在同一原生规律内都有完成后缀。静态清单会遗漏实际占用，释放／形成可恢复或建立准备；无限允许调度仍可能永不推进目标。 全体有限一阶身份、生成和接合真值一致，甚至有一个保留任意有限参数公式的映射，仍不足以迫出全范围可达结构的原生同构；Cantor 对角线给出真实不同构对手。保真映射再有全局覆盖则恢复同构。 | Retains calibration, structural rivals, compositional uniqueness and native unfolding’s joining/substitution, branch-continuation distinctions and same-law error cases. Adds empty-history probes, unique units and transport, an exact completion criterion and state-interface recovery, retaining capacity and internal-word-loss counters. The latest concrete summary-sufficiency proof derives an effective law and exact finite-history correspondence from the unchanged mechanism, without a separate Exact or homomorphism premise. The universal attempt characterizes arbitrary ternary-relation recovery: complete success answers and minimum states/edges still allow nonisomorphic mechanisms; deterministic reachable continuation-separated realizations recover uniqueness. The continuation derives finite-branch bisimulation from compound questions at shared conditions and reconstructs code histories lifting from every compatible native start; scoped structural uniqueness no longer requires determinism. Successor compactness further replaces finite branching, preserving structural correspondence and lifting whole infinite code runs; a genuinely infinitely branching positive model contrasts with equal finite-modal answers but different infinite realization. Joint questions further force step/joint correspondence under explicit correlated-pair compactness and lift finite nodewise plans; acquisition/release derives shared-support conflict. Complete isolated joint profiles can still lose designated-part aliasing, recovered by fixed-partner questions in the finite witness. Complete descendant support and sibling exclusion further connect arbitrary finite plans to distinct global requests and a common holding history; omission failures and a positive repair are checked. Every fixed n-ary success list can still miss an (n+1)-ary capacity restriction. Formation, withdrawal, acquisition and release have exact certificate-preservation conditions; every permitted finite history has a completing suffix in the same native law. Static lists can lose actual occupation, release/formation renew readiness, and an infinite permitted schedule can still never progress. All finite first-order identity, step and joint truths, even preserved by one coherent elementary map on every finite source tuple, do not force native isomorphism of wholly root-reachable worlds. | F30 | 对齐接续的形式与执行包<br>Formal and execution package for alignment continuity | 核验有限稳健策略选择、实际命令、继承改革历史及市场到载体的精确细化；同一形式包链接两次不重复计为独立成果。 后续实际检查形成未预列的纠正地址；局部成功见证与给定范围的穷尽分开，重新核验当前配置与权限。 再接续将相关故障范围分别用于观察与委托许可；取得真实路线、保持委托范围并接到付费执行及防重放。 本轮由明确共同原因与公式计算故障范围，再接入保留委托、新权限版本和纠正预算的原子组织改革。 再将保留的多个因果说明汇成共同认证范围，按相关观察修订并实际纠正；成功可保留未定区别，也可直接使用当前充分证据。 再将事实答案与见证失陷共同解释，保留相容候选；无需指定绝对可信者，明确可辨认条件并接入实际纠正。 再按控制与当前失陷的可能变化展开成对候选，以顺序报告修订，并将合法有限历史的当前覆盖接入实际纠正。 | Checks finite robust policy synthesis, concrete commands, the inherited reform history and exact market-to-carrier refinement; cross-linking the same package does not count it twice. Temporal continuation adds 34 theorems / 66 audits and 35,486 matched records (32 empty dependencies). Instantaneous fault bounds do not bound the collection window; moving corruption can produce all-false reports. ## 六、原话、评审与证据入口 / Directives, reviews and evidence

| 编号 / ID | 理论或命题 / Theory or proposition | 中文论断 | English formulation | 范围与状态 / Scope and status | 原文、证明与评审 / Sources, proofs and reviews |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| E01 | Ren 的非二元方法与生生方向<br>Ren’s nondual method and generative direction | 原话规定从同一生成展开解释关系分化，保留多个“生”的研究范围，并正面研究生生的伦理方向。 | The directives require explaining differentiation within one unfolding, retaining multiple ranges of life/formation and investigating Shengsheng as a favorable direction. | 用户研究指令与理论来源；指令本身不计作证明。<br>User research directives and theoretical sources; instructions are not proofs. | [非二元指令 / Nondual directive](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-06-project-wide-nondual-method.md)<br>[生生方向原话 / Generative direction](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-shengsheng-ethical-direction.md) |
| E02 | 零公理与显式前提两条路线的来源<br>Sources for zero-axiom and explicit-premise routes | 保留“生成重要”的显式起点研究，同时按当前要求继续更强的零公理理论；两条路线分别报告完成范围。 | Retain inquiry from an explicit generation-matters premise while pursuing the stronger zero-axiom theory; report their achievements separately. | 原话与执行范围；不能把一条路线的结果当成另一条已完成。<br>Direct messages and execution scope; success of one route does not complete the other. | [公理与未来 / Axiom and future](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-generation-axiom-and-future.md)<br>[零公理继续指令 / Zero-axiom continuation](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-07-zero-axiom-complete-classification.md)<br>[本表及维护请求 / This index and its upkeep](../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-08-bilingual-generativity-chain-index.md) |
| E03 | 条件性统一基础的完成审查<br>Completion review of the conditional foundation | 共同基础已在显式根据、相连推导、组合、生成/退化解释和区分性支持五项原定条件下被接受。 | The conditional foundation has been accepted under its five original criteria: explicit grounds, connected derivation, composition, constructive/destructive regimes and discriminating support. | 声明范围内完成；普遍经验真理、唯一必要性和零假设根据仍开放。<br>Complete at its declared scope; universal empirical truth, unique necessity and assumption-free grounding remain open. | [五项完成审查 / Five-condition review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/FIXED-FINISH-LINE-AUDIT.md#15-completion-assessment-of-the-conditional-candidate) |
| E04 | 规范根据的正面论证与实质反审<br>Affirmative grounding arguments and substantive counterreview | 同一完整关系须接受正面解释、保留其事实的强对手和证据评估；思想案例与形式反例各有范围。 | The same complete relationship faces an affirmative explanation, a substantive rival preserving its facts, and evidence assessment; thought cases and formal counterexamples retain their scopes. | 研究评审与来源入口；不把评审次数或原文引用数当作证明。<br>Research review and source entry points; neither review counts nor citation counts constitute proof. | [优先研究整合 / Priority integration](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-INTEGRATION.md)<br>[原始文献评估 / Primary-source assessment](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/NORMATIVE-WARRANT-SOURCES.md)<br>[本轮实质反审 / Current substantive review](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-REVIEW.md)<br>[Sen 阅读与局限 / Sen reading and limits](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/ZERO-AXIOM-CLASSIFICATION-SOURCES.md) |
| E05 | 良知的经典与发展证据<br>Classical and developmental evidence for Liangzhi | 经典文本、社会形成与发展研究为具体机制和解释提供比较；形成动机的证据不直接证明伦理有效性。 | Classical texts and studies of social formation and development constrain particular mechanisms and interpretations; evidence for motivation formation does not directly establish ethical validity. | 各来源按原阅读和研究范围使用；不是新经验实验。<br>Each source retains its recorded reading and evidential scope; no new empirical experiment. | [演化与发展来源 / Evolution and development sources](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIANGZHI-EVOLUTION-SOURCES.md)<br>[王阳明原典范围 / Wang Yangming source scope](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/WANG-YANGMING-COMPLETION-SOURCES.md)<br>[实践整合 / Practical integration](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIANGZHI-PRACTICAL-INTEGRATION.md) |
| E06 | 实际对应与证据范围<br>Real-world correspondence and evidential scope | 模型、合成检验、实际观察与哲学根据承担不同工作，保留来源谱系及反例。生命证据连接维护、功能恢复、有效组织及后续参与；区分可投入能力、推断参与、替代损失的生产和净增长。转变研究区分材料保留与变化后的要求；中断形成的谱系实验和生长中单个区室的对照进一步检验既有供给何时不足，支持三类机制的整合。 | Models, synthetic checks, observation and philosophical warrant do different work, preserving provenance and counterexamples. Living evidence connects maintenance, functional recovery, effective organization and later participation, distinguishing recruitable capacity, inferred engagement, replacement production and net growth. Transition studies separate material retention from changed requirements; interrupted-formation lineages and a growing-compartment contrast further test inherited insufficiency, supporting the three-mode synthesis. | 一般生命说明的证据整合已获审查，不等于全部现实均已验证。量恢复不证明全部功能；受损制备可保留残余活性。不同物种、版本和制备不合并成同一历史；分裂停止不等于死亡，荧光不等于精确有效副本数。计数增加不等于功能充分，当前供给不等于历次出生数。批次无物理时长，平均速率不自动成为逐历史上界；普遍更新期限与伦理权威均未证明。<br>The evidence synthesis for the general living account is reviewed, without verifying the entire real domain. Amount restoration does not prove full function; impaired preparations can retain residual activity. Different organisms, versions and preparations are not one history; division arrest is not death, and fluorescence is not an exact functional-copy count. Count increase is not functional sufficiency, and current provision is not cumulative births. Batches supply no physical duration; mean rates do not automatically bound every history. Universal renewal deadlines and ethical authority remain unproved. | [经验范围 / Empirical scope](../unified-2026-10-05/EMPIRICAL-SCOPE.md)<br>[来源图 / Source map](../unified-2026-10-05/SOURCE-MAP.md)<br>[细项主张 / Claim register](../unified-2026-10-05/CLAIMS.md)<br>[生命机制与原始研究 / Living mechanisms and primary studies](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-DEPENDENCY-MECHANISMS.md)<br>[恢复证据与对应范围 / Recovery evidence and correspondence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-RECOVERY-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[保留组织与实际功能证据 / Retained organization and functional evidence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-ORGANIZATION-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[部分能力、参与与周转证据 / Partial capacity, engagement and turnover evidence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-CAPACITY-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[生命转变原始证据评估 / Living-transition primary-evidence assessment](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-TRANSITION-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[转变评审与既有证明对应 / Transition review and existing-proof correspondence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-TRANSITION-REVIEW.md)<br>[完整目标验收及未证范围 / Full-goal completion audit and open claims](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-DEPENDENCY-INTEGRATION.md#12-full-goal-completion-audit)<br>[扩展、形成中断与不足证据 / Expansion, interrupted formation and insufficiency evidence](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-EXPANSION-EVIDENCE.md)<br>[独立评审及完整说明验收 / Independent review and general-account acceptance](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/LIVING-EXPANSION-REVIEW.md) |

## 持续维护 / Ongoing maintenance

这份表在原位置持续更新。关联研究发生实质变化时，同时修订中英文论断、状态与链接。未发现相关变化时不改写内容，也不重复运行已通过的证明。

Maintain this same document in place. When linked research changes substantively, revise both languages, status and links together. Without relevant changes, leave the content and successful proof runs untouched.

生成文件的编辑来源是 [双语数据](data/generativity-chain.json)。源文件摘要只用于发现变化，不能代替重读与判断；维护流程和更新记录见 [维护记录 / Maintenance record](../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/root-foundation/GENERATIVITY-CHAIN-INDEX-RECORD.md)。

The [bilingual data](data/generativity-chain.json) is the editable source. Source hashes detect changes; they do not replace reading or judgment. Follow the linked maintenance record for the maintenance workflow and update history.
